But “deserve bad things”… I’m not sure I’d put it that way.\n\nDoctor: When you feel depressed, do you believe the neighbors are watching you because you’ve done something wrong or deserve to be punished?\n\nPatient: I don’t know if it’s punishment exactly. I just… I know the people in my building are watching when I come and go, and there’s notes getting slipped to the office about me. People say they’re just regular notices on the doors, but it doesn’t feel that way.\n\nDoctor: Have people told you that your face looks blank or that you show very little emotion?\n\nPatient: I don’t know… nobody’s really said that to me, not that I remember. I don’t pay much attention to my face.\n\nDoctor: Have you lost motivation to do everyday things, like showering, cleaning, or getting things done?\n\nPatient: Yeah… I guess so. I used to go to choir practice, play bridge, cook on Sundays… help people with little repairs. Now I mostly just let the phone ring and tell myself there’s no point going.\n\nDoctor: Even during times when you were not feeling depressed, did you still have very little motivation to do everyday things?\n\nPatient: I’m not really sure. I mean… even when I’m not feeling that low, I don’t always do much. But I don’t know if that’s the same thing or just me being tired of everything.\n\n[Instruction]\n- Ask about exactly ONE thing.
